Minor cautions

JavaScript analysis reported javascript.credential-to-network

Minor caution · medium confidence

The script combines password-based login with browser networking because that is how the live test operates. The excerpt does not prove theft, but the broader login and cookie handling should remain tightly limited to the intended server.

Technical evidence

Scanner reason: JavaScript analysis matched static JavaScript security signal javascript.credential-to-network in this repository.

Contextual assessment: The candidate is a same-representation correlation between credential-bearing environment reads and network-capable browser/authentication operations. Context shows an explicitly configured SillyTavern base URL, authentication, cookie installation, and same-origin session verification; it does not establish an unrelated destination, attacker control, or exfiltration.

Impact: low · Exploitability: unlikely

Developer action: Confirm that authentication responses and cookies are used only for the configured SillyTavern base URL, and avoid logging credential or session values.

JavaScript analysis reported javascript.xray.unsafe-regex

Minor caution · medium confidence

This is a pattern used to recognize command-like roleplay text. Very unusual or extremely long input might make matching slower, but the evidence does not show a practical denial-of-service condition.

Technical evidence

Scanner reason: JavaScript analysis matched static JavaScript security signal javascript.xray.unsafe-regex in this repository.

Contextual assessment: The scanner reports an unsafe-regex signal for a production classifier expression. Supplied context shows a bounded-prefix command recognizer using a static alternation and word boundary, invoked during text classification, but provides no timing data, input limits, event reachability, or demonstrated catastrophic backtracking. Exposure and material impact are therefore not demonstrated.

Impact: low · Exploitability: plausible

Developer action: Consider simplifying or bounding the command-matching expression and test it against long adversarial inputs if utility classification can process attacker-controlled chat text.

JavaScript analysis reported javascript.xray.unsafe-regex

Minor caution · medium confidence

This pattern helps identify operational dialogue in quoted roleplay text. It could deserve performance testing with large messages, but the supplied evidence does not prove that users can cause harmful slowdowns.

Technical evidence

Scanner reason: JavaScript analysis matched static JavaScript security signal javascript.xray.unsafe-regex in this repository.

Contextual assessment: The scanner identifies a regex in production text classification. The shown logic extracts quoted segments with explicit length bounds and applies additional static tests. No measured worst-case behavior, attacker-controlled unbounded workload, or service-level denial of service is supplied, so this remains a low-confidence performance concern rather than a demonstrated vulnerability.

Impact: low · Exploitability: plausible

Developer action: Review the quoted-segment and directive-detection expressions for bounded processing, and add adversarial performance tests for long roleplay messages.

Minor caution · medium confidence

Technical evidence

Scanner reason: Gitleaks matched secret-detection rule generic-api-key in this repository.

Contextual assessment: The candidate is in a tooling test and the matched value is redacted. No supplied evidence establishes that it is a live credential, reaches an external destination, or is used by shipped runtime behavior. The surrounding test context includes deliberately raw-looking fixture values and assertions that sensitive raw content is not persisted, but it does not expose the matched value's exact role.

Impact: low · Exploitability: unlikely

Developer action: Review line 682 to determine whether the matched value is a real current credential; remove it or replace it with a clearly inert test fixture, and rotate it if usable.

Minor caution · medium confidence

This tooling accepts passwords for test accounts and performs browser/network activity, but the supplied evidence describes a local multi-user readiness probe and does not show those passwords being uploaded to an outside service.

Technical evidence

Scanner reason: JavaScript analysis matched static JavaScript security signal javascript.credential-to-network in this repository.

Contextual assessment: The shown code reads per-user or shared test passwords from environment variables and the usage text describes Playwright interaction with a configured SillyTavern base URL. The supplied window does not establish a credential-to-network data flow to an unintended destination; the scanner correlation is insufficient to demonstrate theft. Because the full network and artifact-writing implementation is not supplied, credential handling warrants review rather than a material finding.

Impact: low · Exploitability: unlikely

Developer action: Review the complete script around credential handling and network calls; ensure live probe credentials are never logged, serialized into artifacts, or sent anywhere except the configured local SillyTavern instance.
